# PERL-PRIVATE-FACTORY-CAPTURE: Actual factory and dependency emission capture

## Metadata

- Tree ID: `PERL-PRIVATE-FACTORY-CAPTURE`
- Status: `active` / metadata .3.2.1.12 next
- Roadmap lane: `.spec language evolution / producer-bound residual repair`
- Created: `2026-10-10`
- Last updated: `2026-10-19`
- Owner: LinkedSpec repo-local PNT
- Parent: `SPEC-LANGUAGE-SELF-CONTAINMENT.1.3.3.0.7.2.2.0.3.1.1.1.2.1.3` in `docs/tasks/SPEC-LANGUAGE-SELF-CONTAINMENT.md`
- Planning owner: `SPEC-LANGUAGE-SELF-CONTAINMENT.1.3.3.0.7.2.2.0.3.1.1.1.2.1.3.0`

## Goal

Implement the selected private actual-callback registration and separate injected dependency capture protocol.
Keep public callback references, dependency/context maps, ordinary values, contexts, aliases, effects, error objects
and canonical family order intact. This linked implementation tree preserves every existing stable parent ID;
no existing task content or machine consumer moves.

## Scope and boundaries

The selected protocol and original opaque dependency diagnosis remain canonical in
`docs/checkpoints/SPEC-LANGUAGE-SELF-CONTAINMENT.1.3.3.0.7.2.2.0.3.1.1.1.2.1.0-plan.json` and
`docs/knowledge/perl-private-input-authority-selection.md`. Detailed proposed owner/API boundaries are in
`docs/checkpoints/SPEC-LANGUAGE-SELF-CONTAINMENT.1.3.3.0.7.2.2.0.3.1.1.1.2.1.3.0-plan.json`. Planning introduces no runtime measurement or new behavior.

Known callbacks come from actual built-in factory references in the current original-source/producer scope.
Opaque dependencies receive separate captures and cannot donate nested publications to an outer callback.
Private lexical thunks may retain and invoke the actual callback only while caller maps and public returned
callback fields stay exact. List and void calls retain native contexts under publication-disabled boundaries;
only scalar calls use the existing scalar-string packet capture. Trusted later output builders must explicitly
retain and compose child captures; ordinary values alone transfer no packet.

Typed/raw output construction, packet composition, original interval mapping, complete private Phase0 and
independent canonical admission stay with the original parent owners. The current replacement algorithm and
quoted-first example do not change here. Other repositories and all submodules remain read-only black boxes.

```python
# Final consolidated draft installation
import base64, gzip, hashlib, json, subprocess
from pathlib import Path
root = Path.cwd()
prefix = 'docs/checkpoints/pipeline-scope/'
route = 'docs/checkpoints/PERL-PRIVATE-FACTORY-CAPTURE.6-knowledge-route/'
claim_path = 'docs/claims/PERL-PRIVATE-FACTORY-CAPTURE.6.json'
sha = lambda data: hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()
assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], text=True).strip() == '64b66167c2277533c2393899e3754e14a74f0b31'
assert not (root / (prefix + 'preserved.json.gz')).exists()
preflight = json.loads((root / (prefix + 'publication-preflight.json')).read_bytes())
value = base64.b64decode(preflight['draft_base64'])
assert preflight['result'] == 'PASS' and preflight['bytes'] == len(value) == 120239 <= 131072
assert sha(value) == preflight['draft_sha256'] == '9ef77e85f62ed8bcce0551760e261186257837a6499f7e3ff706367274d7b54b'
assert sha((root / (prefix + 'publish.py')).read_bytes()) == preflight['producer_sha256']
record = json.loads(value)
assert record['owner'] == 'PERL-PRIVATE-FACTORY-CAPTURE.6'
assert record['claims'][0]['durability']['mode'] == 'live'
bindings = record['claims'][0]['durability']['inputs']
manifest = json.loads((root / (route + 'manifest.json')).read_bytes())
expected = set(manifest['inputs']) - {manifest['deleted_card']}
expected.update(path.relative_to(root).as_posix() for path in (root / route).iterdir() if path.is_file())
assert len(bindings) == preflight['input_count'] == len(expected) == 711
assert {item['path'] for item in bindings} == expected == set(preflight['input_modes'])
for item in bindings:
    source = root / item['path']
    assert source.is_file() and not source.is_symlink()
    assert sha(source.read_bytes()) == item['sha256'], item['path']
    assert source.stat().st_mode & 0o7777 == preflight['input_modes'][item['path']]
standard = json.loads(gzip.decompress((root / 'docs/checkpoints/PERL-PRIVATE-FACTORY-CAPTURE.6-standard-verification.json.gz').read_bytes()))
original = next(item for item in standard['sources'] if item['path'] == claim_path)
target = root / claim_path
assert target.read_bytes() == base64.b64decode(original['base64'])
assert target.stat().st_mode & 0o7777 == manifest['claim_mode']
target.write_bytes(value)
assert target.read_bytes() == value
print('Guarded consolidated publication installed:711 inputs;120239 bytes')
```

bash tools/project_data_run.sh python3 - <<'PY'
import base64, gzip, hashlib, json, subprocess
from pathlib import Path
root = Path.cwd()
prefix = 'docs/checkpoints/pipeline-scope/'
artifacts = 'docs/checkpoints/PERL-PRIVATE-FACTORY-CAPTURE.6-artifacts/'
claim_path = 'docs/claims/PERL-PRIVATE-FACTORY-CAPTURE.6.json'
raw_path = prefix + 'preserved.json.gz'
sha = lambda value: hashlib.sha256(value).hexdigest()
assert subprocess.check_output(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], text=True).strip() == '64b66167c2277533c2393899e3754e14a74f0b31'
assert not (root / raw_path).exists()
preflight = json.loads((root / (prefix + 'publication-preflight.json')).read_bytes())
assert preflight['result'] == 'PASS'
value = base64.b64decode(preflight['draft_base64'])
assert sha(value) == preflight['draft_sha256'] and len(value) == preflight['bytes'] <= 131072
record = json.loads(value)
assert record['owner'] == 'PERL-PRIVATE-FACTORY-CAPTURE.6'
bindings = record['claims'][0]['durability']['inputs']
assert len(bindings) == preflight['input_count'] == 696 and len(value) == 117298
assert sha((root / (prefix + 'publish.py')).read_bytes()) == preflight['producer_sha256']
manifest = json.loads((root / (artifacts + 'manifest.json')).read_bytes())
expected = set(manifest['inputs']) - {raw_path}
expected.update(path.relative_to(root).as_posix() for path in (root / artifacts).iterdir() if path.is_file())
expected.add('docs/knowledge/pipeline-checkpoint-artifact-transport.md')
assert {item['path'] for item in bindings} == expected == set(preflight['input_modes'])
for item in bindings:
    source = root / item['path']
    assert source.is_file() and not source.is_symlink()
    assert sha(source.read_bytes()) == item['sha256']
    assert source.stat().st_mode & 0o7777 == preflight['input_modes'][item['path']]
standard = json.loads(gzip.decompress((root / 'docs/checkpoints/PERL-PRIVATE-FACTORY-CAPTURE.6-standard-verification.json.gz').read_bytes()))
before = next(item for item in standard['sources'] if item['path'] == claim_path)
assert (root / claim_path).read_bytes() == base64.b64decode(before['base64'])
(root / claim_path).write_bytes(value)
assert (root / claim_path).read_bytes() == value
print('Reviewed pipeline publication installed:696 inputs;117298 bytes')
